Function `shape_cd(a, M, n_iters, alpha, ridge)`:

def theta_wrapper(x, p, ref, tmp=None):
    n = len(x)+1

C-domain function details:
```
def spectral_c_cycle(a, n_iters=200, factor_start=0.98, factor_end=0.90, alpha=0.7, rng=None, mode='clip'):
    a = nonnegative normalized
    n=len; N=nfft(2n-1); Mlen=2n-1
    best=a; best_sc=score
    A_old = rfft(a,N)
    for it:
       progress...
       target_factor = factor_start + (factor_end-factor_start)*progress
       F = rfft(a,N)  # recompute to align with current a; or use A_old
       B = F*F
       C = irfft(B,N)[:Mlen]; C=max(C,0); S=C.sum(); if S<... break
       C = C/S (should sum 1)
       Mcur = C.max()
       target = max(Mcur*target_factor, 1e-12)
       Cproj=C.copy()
       high=C>target
       Cproj[high] = target * (C[high]/target)**(1-alpha)
       # maybe normalize Cproj sum to 1? If not, its sum <1. We can normalize to sum 1 by scaling: Cproj /= Cproj.sum(). But then peaks >target by factor. If target close M, scale c ~1; okay. Use scale maybe c = min(1, target / Cproj.max())? Hmm.
       Cproj = Cproj / Cproj.sum()
       # If max still high, repeat; but okay.
       Br = rfft(Cproj,N)
       # A_new magnitude sqrt(abs(Br)), phase from F
       amp = np.sqrt(np.maximum(np.abs(Br),0))
       # phase: We can use angle(F) + phase_noise*random? For deterministic use F.
       phase = np.angle(F)
       A_new = amp * np.exp(1j*phase)
       anew = irfft(A_new,N)
       anew = anew[:n]
       # positive projection with smoothing: if many negative, use max; if sum small, fallback to previous + small noise
       if anew.sum() <= 0: anew = np.abs(anew)
       anew = np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
       Snew=anew.sum()
       if Snew < 1e-12: break
       anew /= Snew
       sc=score(anew)
       if sc<best...
       A_old = rfft(anew,N)
       a = anew
```
This is O(FFT). Could run 100-300 iterations.

Implement `theta_logscore_full` carefully for performance:

def theta_logscore_full(theta, p):
    n = len(theta)
    maxth = float(np.max(theta))
    b = np.exp(theta - maxth)
    S = float(b.sum())
    if S <= 0: return 1e300, np.zeros(n)

    idx = Mlen-1 - np.arange(n)  # length n
    flow = 2.0 * convRB[idx]
    a_norm = b/S
    g = flow - 2.0*a_norm
    return f, g
```

def c_shape_cycle(a, n_iters=200, factor_start=0.99, factor_end=0.90, alpha=0.6, phase_mode=0):
    a = np.maximum(a,0); S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12: a=np.ones(n)/n else a/=S
    n=len(a); Mlen=2*n-1; N=nfft_len(3*n-2)? For C only need nfft_len(Mlen), but use same? For speed use C_N=nfft_len(Mlen). A=rfft(a,C_N). C=irfft(A*A)[:Mlen]. For new A, use N2 maybe C_N; a_new length n from IFFT. Use C_N. Gap okay.
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
    for it in range(n_iters):
       progress=...
       target_factor=factor_start+(factor_end-factor_start)*progress
       F=np.fft.rfft(a,C_N)
       C=np.fft.irfft(F*F,C_N)[:Mlen]
       C=np.maximum(C,0.0)
       S=C.sum(); if S<... break; C=C/S
       Mcur=C.max()
       target=Mcur*target_factor
       if target<=1e-300: break
       Cp=C.copy()
       high=Cp>target
       if high.any():
           ratio=Cp[high]/target
           Cp[high]=target*(ratio** (1.0-alpha))
       # Could apply a floor: Cp=np.maximum(Cp, 1e-300)
       Cp_sum=Cp.sum(); if Cp_sum<... break; Cp=Cp/Cp_sum
       Br=np.fft.rfft(Cp,C_N)
       if phase_mode==0: # sqrt
          Anew=np.sqrt(Br+0j)  # Br complex
       else:
          amp=np.sqrt(np.abs(Br))
          ph=np.angle(F)
          Anew=amp*np.exp(1j*ph)
       anew=np.fft.irfft(Anew,C_N)
       anew=anew[:n]
       if phase_mode==1:
           # maybe use positive-part with a small prior to avoid collapse: anew = np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
           pass
       anew=np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
       Snew=anew.sum()
       if Snew<1e-12:
           # fallback: use previous a with tiny noise
           anew = a * (1.0 + 1e-3)
           Snew=anew.sum()
       anew /= Snew
       sc=compute_score_fast(anew)
       if sc<best_sc: update
       a=anew
    return best_a, best_sc
```

def coarse_lp_search(f, n_iters=30, t=0.25, method='highs'):
   n=len(f); f normalized; M=current max
   for it:
      g=lp_max_sum(f,M)
      sumg=g.sum(); if sumg<=1: break
      g_norm=g/sumg
      f_new=(1-t)*f + t*g_norm
      f_new=max(f_new,0); normalize
      M_new=max conv
      if M_new < M: f=f_new, M=M_new else maybe reduce t

Need downsample function:
```
def downsample_sum(a, m):
    n=len(a)
    if n==m: return a
    # bin sum preserving sum
    idx = (np.arange(n) * m) // n
    out = np.bincount(idx, weights=a, minlength=m)
    # length m
    S=out.sum(); if S>0 out/=S
    return out
```
This sums adjacent-ish bins. Could also use cumulative interpolation. Bin sum preserves support and maybe LP nice. For upsample from coarse to full, linear interp. Also include direct downsample sum for full? If parent longer to coarse.

Upsample function:
```
def upsample_interp(a, m):
    n=len(a)
    if n==m: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    x_new=(np.arange(m, dtype=float)+0.5)*n/m
    # maybe include endpoints? Use np.interp clamps.
    b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    S=b.sum(); if S<...
    return b/S
```
This treats a as samples at grid centers. For m>n, linear. Good. Could also use quantile to preserve mass exactly: `cdfa=np.cumsum(a)/a.sum(); cdftarget=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m; b=np.diff(np.interp(cdftarget, cdf_a, np.arange(n+1)))` but if a has flat zeros, interpolation of cdf may yield nonnegative. This preserves integral and can distribute mass across zeros? For zero segments, cdf constant causing vertical jumps; interp may allocate. Might be better to preserve mass. ```
def soft_max_gradient_iteration(a, n_iters=1000, beta=200, lr=0.1, decay=...):
   a=positive normalized
   for it:

def conv_fft_full(a):

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 0:
        return np.ones_like(a) * (1.0/len(a))
    return a / S
```
If floor >0, sum normalization okay.

`conv_fft_full` use real FFT; if n=1, N=1.
```
def conv_fft_full(a):
    n=len(a)
    Mlen=2*n-1
    N=nfft_len(Mlen)
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    C=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)
    C=C[:Mlen]
    S=float(a.sum())
    return C,S
```
Potential small negative; okay.

`compute_score`:
```
def compute_score(a):
    C,S=conv_fft_full(a)
    if S<=1e-300: return np.inf
    Cmax=float(np.max(C))
    if Cmax<0: Cmax=0.0
    return 2.0*len(a)*Cmax/(S*S)
```

`downsample_sum`:
```
def downsample_sum(a,m):
    n=len(a)
    if n==m: return a.copy()
    if m<=0: return a.copy()
    # or if n < m use upsample
    if n < m:
       return upsample_interp(a,m)
    counts? Use bincount weights.
    idx = np.floor(np.arange(n)*m/ n).astype(np.int64)
    out=np.bincount(idx, weights=a, minlength=m)
    return normalize_nonneg(out)
```
If some bins zero. Good.

`upsample_interp`:
```
def upsample_interp(a,m):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=len(a)
    if n==m: return a
    if m<=1: return np.array([sum(a)]) # not
    x_old=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    # map new centers to old centers, clamped
    x_new=(np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)+0.5)*(n-1)/m if n>1 else np.zeros(m)
    # If using center grid: old centers? For n, positions 0..n-1. New centers should span same length. Use (i+0.5)*n/m mapped to old coordinates 0..n-1? Simpler: old = (arange(n)+0.5), new=(arange(m)+0.5)*n/m. Interp clamps to old endpoints.
    x_old = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)+0.5
    x_new = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)+0.5)*(n/m)
    b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    S=b.sum()
    if S<=1e-300:
        b=np.ones(m)/m
        S=1
    return b/S
```
If n=1, x_old [0.5], x_new all 0.5 -> b constant. Need handle n=1.

`generate_seeds`:
```
def generate_seeds(n, rng, parent=None):
    seeds=[]
    if parent is not None and len(parent)>0:
       p=normalize_nonneg(parent)
       if len(p)!=n: p=upsample_interp(p,n)
       seeds.append(p)
       # noisy variants
       for nz in (0.01,0.03):
           pn=p*(1.0+nz*rng.standard_normal(n))
           pn=np.maximum(pn,1e-12); pn/=pn.sum(); seeds.append(pn)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    seeds.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    seeds.append(np.maximum(1.0+0.9*np.sin(2*np.pi*x),1e-3)/sum)
    seeds.append(np.maximum(1.0+0.9*np.sin(2*np.pi*x+np.pi/4),1e-3)/sum)
    seeds.append(np.maximum(1.0+0.5*np.cos(4*np.pi*x),1e-3)/sum)
    # random smooth
    for trial in range(3):
       base=np.ones(n)*0.5
       for _ in range(4):
          f=rng.integers(1,6)
          ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
          am=rng.uniform(0.1,0.4)
          base += am*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph)
       base=np.maximum(base,0.02); base/=base.sum(); seeds.append(base)
    # edge? skip bad
    return seeds
```
Normalize each with floor 1e-6 maybe.

C-cycle:
Use N=nfft_len(2n-1). For n=7000, N=16384 (since 13999). Good.
```
def c_shape_cycle(a, n_iters=150, mode=0, p1_start=0.8, p1_end=0.2, p2=0.5, phase_mode=0):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a)
    n=len(a); Mlen=2*n-1; N=nfft_len(Mlen)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
    for it in range(n_iters):
       pr=it/max(1,n_iters-1)
       if mode==0: # clip peaks
          target_factor = p1_start + (p1_end-p1_start)*pr
          alpha = p2
          F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
          C=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:Mlen]
          C=np.maximum(C,0.0); S=C.sum(); if S... break; C=C/S
          Mcur=C.max(); target=max(Mcur*target_factor,1e-300)
          Cp=C.copy()
          high=Cp>target
          if high.any():
              ratio=Cp[high]/target
              Cp[high] = target*(ratio**(1.0-alpha))
          Cp=np.maximum(Cp,1e-300)
          Cs=Cp.sum(); if Cs...; Cp=Cp/Cs
       elif mode==1: # mix flat
          gamma = p1_start + (p1_end-p1_start)*pr
          F=...; C=...
          q=1.0/Mlen
          Cp=(1.0-gamma)*C + gamma*q
       elif mode==2: # mix with median envelope? q = smooth C? use running mean
          # maybe skip
       # sym Cp = 0.5*(Cp+Cp[::-1])
       Cp=0.5*(Cp+Cp[::-1])
       Cp=np.maximum(Cp,0); Cs=Cp.sum(); if...; Cp=Cp/Cs
       Br=np.fft.rfft(Cp,N)
       if phase_mode==0:
           Anew=np.sqrt(Br+0j)
       else:
           # F from current a already; if mode recalc? F available from above
           amp=np.sqrt(np.abs(Br))
           ph=np.angle(F)
           Anew=amp*np.exp(1j*ph)
       anew=np.fft.irfft(Anew,N)
       anew=anew[:n]
       anew=np.maximum(anew,0.0)
       Snew=anew.sum()
       if Snew<1e-12:
           # fallback: use previous with tiny noise
           anew=a*(1.0+1e-4)
           Snew=anew.sum()
       anew=anew/Snew
       sc=compute_score(anew)
       if sc<best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=anew.copy()
       a=anew
    return best_a, best_sc
```
Need ensure `F` defined in phase_mode=1; for mode maybe not. In loop, F computed for C; keep.

Could C-cycle be slow due to sqrt of complex rfft with dtype complex. Fine.

Potential problem: np.sqrt of Br where Br[0] maybe zero? If Cp sum positive, Br[0]>0. Good.

`lp_max_sum`:
```
def lp_max_sum(f, M):
    n=len(f); Mlen=2*n-1
    A=np.zeros((Mlen,n), dtype=np.float64)
    # fill columns
    f=np.asarray(f,float)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j+n, j]=f
    c=-np.ones(n)
    b=np.full(Mlen, M, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds=(0.0, None)
    res=opt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
    if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
        g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
        g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
        return g
    return f.copy()  # fallback feasible
```
Memory A for n=1024 fine. But for each LP, fill A O(n^2). Could cache A if f changes? f changes each iteration. Not cache.

`logtheta_objective`: Need no closures. It uses global theta_logscore_full.
```
def logtheta_objective(x, p, order, ref):
    n = len(order)+1
    theta = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta[order]=x
    theta[ref]=0.0
    f,g = theta_logscore_full(theta,p)
    gx = g[order]
    return float(f), gx
```

`theta_logscore_full` as described. Let's carefully implement with N for conv and gradient.
```
def theta_logscore_full(theta,p):
    n=len(theta)
    maxth=float(np.max(theta))
    b=np.exp(theta - maxth)
    S=float(b.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n)
    Mlen=2*n-1
    N=nfft_len(Mlen + n -1)  # 3n-2
    F=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    C=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:Mlen]
    C=np.maximum(C,1e-300)
    logs = p * np.log(C)
    ml=float(np.max(logs))
    t=np.exp(logs - ml)
    tot=float(t.sum())
    if tot <=1e-300:
        return 1e300, np.zeros(n)
    L=ml + np.log(tot)/p
    f=np.log(2.0*n) + L - 2.0*np.log(S)
    w=t/tot
    r=w/C
    Rr=r[::-1]
    FR=np.fft.rfft(Rr,N)
    convRB=np.fft.irfft(F*FR,N)
    idx=Mlen-1 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    flow=2.0 * convRB[idx]
    a_norm=b/S
    g=flow - 2.0*a_norm
    # if any nonfinite, climit?
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
       # fallback subgradient? return large
    return float(f), g
```
Check N: `Mlen+n-1=3n-2`. If n=1, Mlen=1, N=nfft_len(1)=1. idx=0. Works.

Potential issue: F*FR in grad: FR is rfft of Rr length Mlen. But Rr length Mlen, F length N from b length n. Convolution length n+Mlen-1; N enough. Good.

But we used `F=np.fft.rfft(b,N)` for b length n. `FR=np.fft.rfft(Rr,N)`. Product inverse gives linear conv of b and Rr length up to 3n-2. Good.

Performance: In objective, for p=2, C logs and t; Rr w/C. Fine.

Should we subtract f offset for L-BFGS? L-BFGS line search needs approximate function values; offset not problem. But f may be large for p high; use logs. If f nonfinite, return 1e100.

Bounds: If theta max can be >0, b=exp(theta - max), S maybe small if only ref positive? If all x=-40, ref=0, b_ref=1, others ~4e-18, S≈1; C maybe 1; f=log2n + log1 -0 = ~ln(2n) ~9.7. If x large positive, maxθ large, b_ref small, S maybe ~1 (the max bin), f maybe. Good.

`log_lbfgs_run`:
```
def log_lbfgs_run(a, p, maxfun, bound=None):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0)
    n=len(a)
    ref=int(np.argmax(a))
    if a[ref] <= 1e-300:
       ref=0
    order = np.concatenate((np.arange(ref), np.arange(ref+1,n))).astype(np.int64)
    theta = np.zeros(n)
    theta[order]=np.log(np.maximum(a[order],1e-30) / max(a[ref],1e-30))
    x0=theta[order]
    if bound is None:
       bound=max(10.0, float(np.max(np.abs(x0)))+5.0)
    bound=min(35.0, bound)
    bounds= [( -bound, bound) ]*(n-1)
    try:
       res=opt.minimize(logtheta_objective, x0, args=(p, order, ref), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': max(maxfun//10,100), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxls':30})
    except Exception:
       res=None
    if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
       theta=np.zeros(n); theta[order]=res.x;
       # maybe check logfun
       b=np.exp(theta - np.max(theta)); b=np.maximum(b,0); b/=b.sum(); return b
    return a
```

`maxiter` maybe maxfun/10. SciPy maxfun maybe maxiter*maxls? Actually maxfun deadline. `maxiter` can be maxfun//5? L-BFGS each iter may multiple funs. Use maxiter=max(50, maxfun//20). But if maxfun 5000, maxiter250, okay. Set options: `maxiter`: max(20, min(2000, maxfun//10)), `maxfun`: maxfun, `maxls`:30.

Let's implement `coarse_lp_search` with internal time and returns best. It can also call compute_score many times (FFT small).
```
def coarse_lp_search(f, max_lp_calls, deadline):
    f=normalize_nonneg(f); n=len(f); best_f=f.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(f)
    steps=[0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0]
    for it in range(max_lp_calls):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20: break
       C,S=conv_fft_full(f); M=max(C)/(S*S)
       Mval=M
       tstart=time.monotonic(); g=lp_max_sum(f,Mval); lptime=time.monotonic()-tstart
       if lptime > 60 and it>0: # maybe break if too slow? continue?
       Sg=g.sum(); if Sg <= 1.0+1e-7: break
       gn=g/Sg
       local_best=f; local_best_sc=best_sc
       for st in steps:
          cand=(1-st)*f + st*gn
          cand=normalize_nonneg(cand)
          sc=compute_score(cand)
          if sc<local_best_sc: local_best=cand; local_best_sc=sc
       if local_best_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          f=local_best; best_f=f.copy(); best_sc=local_best_sc
       else:
          # try smaller steps not in list? break
          break
    return best_f, best_sc
```

Let's write `peak_log_descent` carefully:
```
def peak_log_descent(a, max_iters=400, n_peaks=12, lr_init=0.25, deadline=None):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a, floor=1e-12)
    n=len(a)
    theta=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-30))
    theta -= float(np.mean(theta))
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a)
    lr=lr_init
    Mlen=2*n-1
    K= min(n_peaks, Mlen)
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       b=np.exp(theta - np.max(theta))
       S=float(b.sum())
       if S<=... break
       F=np.fft.rfft(b, nfft_len(Mlen)); C=irFFT(F*F)[:Mlen]
       Mval=float(np.max(C)); sc=2*n*Mval/(S*S)
       if sc<best_sc: update
       if Mval<=1e-300: break
       if n_peaks>=Mlen: active=np.arange(Mlen); w=np.ones(len(active))/len(active)
       else: active=np.argpartition(C, -n_peaks)[-n_peaks:]
           ca=C[active]
           # weights proportional to ca (or ca/M)
           w=ca/ ca.sum()
       flow=np.zeros(n)
       for idx,k in enumerate(active):
          k=int(k); ck=float(C[k]); if ck<=0 continue; r=w[idx]/ck
          js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(k,n-1)
          if je>=js:
             start_idx=k-je; end_idx=k-js
             flow[js:je+1] += 2.0*r*b[start_idx:end_idx+1][::-1]
       grad=flow - 2.0*(b/S)
       # step
       accepted=False
       for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625):
           trial=theta - lr*fac*grad
           # center and clip
           trial -= float(np.mean(trial))
           if np.any(trial>80) or np.any(trial<-80):
               trial=np.clip(trial,-80,80)
           bt=np.exp(trial - np.max(trial))
           st=bt.sum()
           if st<=...: continue
           sat=bt/st
           sc2=compute_score(sat)
           if sc2 < sc - 1e-15:  # require improvement over current
               theta=trial; lr*=1.05? maybe keep; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           lr*=0.5
           if lr<1e-5: break
       else:
           # adapt lr
           if sc2 < sc*0.999: lr=min(1.0, lr*1.1)
    best_a=normalize_nonneg(best_a)
    return best_a, best_sc
```
Use current `sc` for accept. We stored best. If no improvement, reduce lr. Could accept if sc2<best_sc? But line search should compare current. Use current.

```python
"""
Search for a low-peak non-negative coefficient sequence.

The algorithm combines:
- coarse linear-programming fixed-point iterations (the SOTA idea) on small lengths,
- polynomial-square spectral shaping cycles in the convolution domain,
- differentiable log-domain Lp-smoothed objective optimization with L-BFGS-B,
- exact top-peak logarithmic subgradient descent as a final polish.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as opt


def nfft_len(m):
    """Smallest power-of-two FFT length at least m."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << int(m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0):
    """Convert to a non-negative float64 vector and scale sum to 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def conv_fft_full(a):
    """Full linear convolution a*a via FFT. Returns (conv, sum(a))."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    nff = nfft_len(m)
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, nff)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, nff)[:m]
    return c, float(a.sum())


def compute_score(a):
    """Internal score 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a)**2)."""
    c, s = conv_fft_full(a)
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cm):
        cm = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * cm / (s * s))


def downsample_sum(a, m):
    """Downsample by binning as much as possible while preserving total mass."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if m <= 1:
        out = np.array([float(a.sum())], dtype=np.float64)
        return normalize_nonneg(out)
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if n < m:
        return upsample_interp(a, m)
    idx = np.floor(np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) * (m / float(n))).astype(np.int64)
    idx = np.clip(idx, 0, m - 1)
    out = np.bincount(idx, weights=a, minlength=m).astype(np.float64)
    return normalize_nonneg(out)


def upsample_interp(a, m):
    """Upsample by linear interpolation over the step-function samples."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if m <= 1:
        out = np.array([float(a.sum())], dtype=np.float64)
        return normalize_nonneg(out)
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        out = np.full(m, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
        return normalize_nonneg(out)

    old = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    new = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) * (float(n) / float(m))
    b = np.interp(new, old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        b = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    return b / s


def generate_seeds(n, rng, parent=None):
    """Make several simple, diverse non-negative seed sequences."""
    seeds = []

    if parent is not None and parent.size == n:
        p = normalize_nonneg(parent, 1e-12)
        seeds.append(p)
        for nz in (0.01, 0.03, 0.06):
            q = p * (1.0 + nz * rng.standard_normal(n))
            q = np.maximum(q, 1e-12)
            seeds.append(q / q.sum())

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    seeds.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    for ph in (0.0, 0.75, 1.5, 2.25):
        y = np.maximum(1.0 + 0.9 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + ph), 1e-4)
        seeds.append(y / y.sum())

    y = np.maximum(1.0 + 0.45 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x), 1e-4)
    seeds.append(y / y.sum())

    y = np.maximum(1.0 + 0.35 * np.cos(6.0 * np.pi * x) +
                   0.25 * np.cos(10.0 * np.pi * x + 0.7), 1e-4)
    seeds.append(y / y.sum())

    for _ in range(4):
        base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * 0.45
        for _ in range(5):
            frr = int(rng.integers(1, 8))
            pha = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.38))
            base += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * frr * x + pha)
        base = np.maximum(base, 0.02)
        seeds.append(base / base.sum())

    return seeds[:12]


def c_shape_cycle(a, n_iters=120, mode=0, p1_start=0.85, p1_end=0.20,
                  p2=0.50, phase_mode=0):
    """
    Iteratively project the convolution C = a*a toward a flatter profile,
    take a square root of the modified C-spectrum, inverse transform, and
    project back to a non-negative length-n sequence.
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    nff = nfft_len(m)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)

    for it in range(n_iters):
        pr = float(it) / float(max(1, n_iters - 1))

        f = np.fft.rfft(a, nff)
        c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, nff)[:m]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        cs = float(c.sum())
        if cs <= 1e-300:
            break
        c = c / cs

        if mode == 0:
            # clip/shrink peaks
            target_factor = p1_start + (p1_end - p1_start) * pr
            target = max(float(c.max()) * target_factor, 1e-300)
            cp = c.copy()
            high = cp > target
            if np.any(high):
                ratio = cp[high] / target
                alpha = float(np.clip(p2, 0.0, 0.999))
                cp[high] = target * (ratio ** (1.0 - alpha))
            cp = np.maximum(cp, 1e-300)
        elif mode == 1:
            # mix with flat target
            gamma = p1_start + (p1_end - p1_start) * pr
            q = 1.0 / float(m)
            cp = (1.0 - gamma) * c + gamma * q
            cp = np.maximum(cp, 1e-300)
        else:
            # mix with local median envelope
            gamma = p1_start + (p1_end - p1_start) * pr
            k = max(21, n // 32 // 2 * 2 + 1)
            kern = np.hanning(k) if k >= 3 else np.ones(k)
            kern = kern / max(float(kern.sum()), 1e-300)
            env = np.convolve(c, kern, mode="same")
            env = np.maximum(env, 1e-300)
            q = gamma * env + (1.0 - gamma) * (1.0 / float(m))
            cp = np.maximum(q, 1e-300)

        # enforce the symmetry that true a*a profiles have
        cp = 0.5 * (cp + cp[::-1])
        cp = np.maximum(cp, 1e-300)
        cs = float(cp.sum())
        if cs <= 1e-300:
            break
        cp = cp / cs

        br = np.fft.rfft(cp, nff)
        br = br.astype(np.complex128, copy=False)

        if phase_mode == 0:
            # principal square root of the target spectrum
            anew_amp = br
        else:
            # keep the old phase, only change magnitude to target spectrum
            anew_amp = np.sqrt(np.abs(br)) * np.exp(1j * np.angle(f))

        if anew_amp.size > 0:
            anew_amp[0] = float(abs(anew_amp[0]))
        if nff % 2 == 0 and anew_amp.size > 0:
            anew_amp[-1] = float(abs(anew_amp[-1]))

        anew = np.fft.irfft(anew_amp, nff)
        anew = anew[:n]
        anew = np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
        sn = float(anew.sum())
        if sn <= 1e-12:
            anew = a + 1e-4
            sn = float(anew.sum())
        anew = anew / sn

        sc = compute_score(anew)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = anew.copy()
        a = anew

    return best_a, best_sc


def lp_max_sum(f, limit=1.0):
    """
    Solve  max 1^T g  s.t. conv(f,g) <= limit, g >= 0.
    f should already be scaled so that limit is usually 1.
    """
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = f.size
    m = 2 * n - 1

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(f)) or float(f.sum()) <= 1e-300:
        return f.copy()

    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    if limit <= 1e-300:
        return f.copy()

    aub = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        aub[j:j + n, j] = f

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.full(m, float(limit), dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        res = opt.linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=aub,
            b_ub=b,
            bounds=(0.0, 1.0e6),
            method="highs",
            options={
                "presolve": True,
                "dual_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
                "primal_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
            }
        )
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(g)) and float(g.sum()) > 0.0:
                return g
    except Exception:
        pass

    return f.copy()


def coarse_lp_search(f, max_lp_calls=12, deadline=None):
    """
    Coarse SOTA LP fixed-point search:
    f -> solve LP for g with conv(f/M,g)<=1 -> normalize g -> move f toward g.
    """
    f = normalize_nonneg(f, 0.0)
    n = f.size
    if deadline is None:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 1e12

    best_f = f.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(f)

    steps = (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 0.1, 0.2)

    for it in range(max_lp_calls):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break

        c, s = conv_fft_full(f)
        mval = float(np.max(c)) / (s * s)
        if mval <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(mval):
            break

        w = f / mval
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
            w = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0e6, neginf=0.0), 0.0, 1.0e6)

        t0 = time.monotonic()
        g = lp_max_sum(w, 1.0)
        lp_time = time.monotonic() - t0

        sg = float(g.sum())
        if sg <= 1.0 + 1e-8:
            break

        gn = g / sg

        local_best = f.copy()
        local_sc = best_sc

        for st in steps:
            cand = (1.0 - st) * f + st * gn
            cand = normalize_nonneg(cand, 0.0)
            sc = compute_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_sc:
                local_sc = sc
                local_best = cand.copy()

        if local_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            f = local_best
            best_f = f.copy()
            best_sc = local_sc
        else:
            # Try one more smaller step if not in the list.
            for st in (0.05, 0.02, 0.01):
                cand = (1.0 - st) * f + st * gn
                cand = normalize_nonneg(cand, 0.0)
                sc = compute_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_sc:
                    local_sc = sc
                    local_best = cand.copy()
            if local_sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                f = local_best
                best_f = f.copy()
                best_sc = local_sc
            else:
                break

    return best_f, best_sc


def theta_logscore_full(theta, p):
    """
    Log-smoothed score for variables theta, where a = exp(theta).
    Minimizes log(2n * Lp-norm(C) / sum(a)^2), p -> inf gives max score.
    Returns (log-score, gradient).
    """
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = theta.size
    if n == 0:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    mt = float(np.max(theta))
    b = np.exp(theta - mt)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    mlen = 2 * n - 1
    # Needed for both full convolution and cross-correlation gradient.
    nff = nfft_len(mlen + n - 1)

    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, nff)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fb * fb, nff)[:mlen]
    c = np.maximum(c, 1.0e-300)

    p = float(p)
    if p < 1.0:
        p = 1.0

    logs = p * np.log(c)
    ml = float(np.max(logs))
    tmp = np.exp(logs - ml)
    tot = float(tmp.sum())
    if tot <= 1.0e-300:
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    ell = ml + float(np.log(tot)) / p
    fval = float(np.log(2.0 * n) + ell - 2.0 * np.log(s))

    w = tmp / tot
    r = w / c

    rr = r[::-1]
    fr = np.fft.rfft(rr, nff)
    conv_rb = np.fft.irfft(fb * fr, nff)
    idx = mlen - 1 - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    flow = 2.0 * conv_rb[idx]

    a_norm = b / s
    grad = flow - 2.0 * a_norm

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        fval = 1.0e100

    return float(fval), grad


def logtheta_objective(x, p, order, ref):
    """Objective for the L-BFGS caller, with reference coordinate fixed to 0."""
    n = len(order) + 1
    theta = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta[order] = x
    theta[ref] = 0.0

    f, g = theta_logscore_full(theta, p)
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    gx = g[order]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        return 1.0e100, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    return float(f), gx


def estimate_theta_eval_time(n, p=2.0):
    """Rough timing for one theta_logscore_full evaluation."""
    theta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(3):
        theta_logscore_full(theta, p)
    dt = (time.monotonic() - t0) / 3.0
    return max(dt, 1.0e-4)


def log_lbfgs_run(a, p, maxfun=3000):
    """Run L-BFGS-B on the log-domain Lp-smoothed objective."""
    a = normalize_nonneg(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 2:
        return a.copy()

    ref = int(np.argmax(a))
    if a[ref] <= 1e-300:
        ref = 0

    order = np.concatenate((np.arange(ref, dtype=np.int64),
                            np.arange(ref + 1, n, dtype=np.int64)))

    safe_a = np.maximum(a, 1e-30)
    theta = np.log(safe_a) - float(np.log(safe_a[ref]))
    x0 = theta[order].astype(np.float64, copy=True)

    bound = max(10.0, float(np.max(np.abs(x0))) + 5.0)
    bound = min(bound, 35.0)
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -bound, bound)

    bounds = [(-bound, bound)] * (n - 1)
    maxiter = max(30, min(5000, maxfun // 8))

    try:
        res = opt.minimize(
            logtheta_objective,
            x0,
            args=(float(p), order, ref),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "ftol": 1e-15,
                "gtol": 1e-11,
                "maxls": 25,
                "disp": False
            }
        )
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            theta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            theta[order] = np.clip(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), -40.0, 40.0)
            b = np.exp(theta - float(np.max(theta)))
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
            s = float(b.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                return b / s
    except Exception:
        pass

    return a.copy()


def peak_log_descent(a, max_iters=350, n_peaks=12, lr_init=0.22, deadline=None):
    """
    Log-domain subgradient descent targeting the largest convolution peaks.
    Uses short backtracking line searches on actual score.
    """
    a = normalize_nonneg(a, 1e-12)
    n = a.size
    if deadline is None:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 1e12
    if n <= 2:
        return a.copy(), compute_score(a)

    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-30))
    theta -= float(np.mean(theta))

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a)

    lr = float(lr_init)
    mlen = 2 * n - 1
    K = min(max(1, int(n_peaks)), mlen)

    for _ in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break

        b = np.exp(theta - float(np.max(theta)))
        s = float(b.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            break

        nff = nfft_len(mlen)
        fb = np.fft.rfft(b, nff)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fb * fb, nff)[:mlen]
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if cmax <= 1e-300:
            break
        sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax / (s * s))
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            break
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        if K >= mlen:
            active = np.arange(mlen, dtype=np.int64)
            ca = c.astype(np.float64, copy=False)
        else:
            active = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:].astype(np.int64)
            ca = c[active].astype(np.float64, copy=False)

        casum = float(ca.sum())
        if casum <= 1e-300:
            wts = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
        else:
            wts = casum and (ca / casum)

        flow = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for ii, kk in enumerate(active):
            kk = int(kk)
            ck = float(c[kk])
            if ck <= 1e-300:
                continue
            r = float(wts[ii]) / ck

            js = max(0, kk - n + 1)
            je = min(kk, n - 1)
            if je >= js:
                start_idx = kk - je
                end_idx = kk - js
                flow[js:je + 1] += 2.0 * r * b[start_idx:end_idx + 1][::-1]

        grad = flow - 2.0 * (b / s)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if gnorm <= 1e-18:
            break

        accepted = False
        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625):
            trial = theta - lr * fac * (grad / (gnorm + 1e-300))
            trial *= gnorm
            trial = theta - lr * fac * grad
            trial -= float(np.mean(trial))
            trial = np.clip(trial, -80.0, 80.0)

            bt = np.exp(trial - float(np.max(trial)))
            stok = float(bt.sum())
            if stok <= 1e-300:
                continue
            at = bt / stok
            sc2 = compute_score(at)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc - 1e-14:
                theta = trial
                a = at
                accepted = True
                if sc2 < sc * 0.999:
                    lr = min(1.0, lr * 1.08)
                break

        if not accepted:
            lr *= 0.5
            if lr < 1e-6:
                break

    best_a = normalize_nonneg(best_a, 0.0)
    return best_a, compute_score(best_a)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    """
    Main search entry point. Keeps a small pool of good candidates, improves them
    with coarse LP templates, convolution-domain shaping, log-domain L-BFGS, and
    final peak subgradient descent.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, min(float(budget_s), 2400.0))
    deadline = t0 + total - 75.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load parent construction if present.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 64:
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(p.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = p / float(p.sum())
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Choose working length.
    if parent is not None and 2048 <= parent.size <= 12000:
        n_full = parent.size
    else:
        n_full = 8192

    parent_full = None
    candidates = []  # list of (score, array)

    if parent is not None:
        if parent.size == n_full:
            parent_full = normalize_nonneg(parent, 0.0)
        else:
            parent_full = upsample_interp(parent, n_full)
        sc = compute_score(parent_full)
        candidates.append((sc, parent_full.copy()))
        print(f"[init] parent length={parent.size} full={n_full} score={sc:.8f}")

    # Always add some generic full-length seeds.
    if parent_full is not None:
        extra = generate_seeds(n_full, rng, parent_full)
    else:
        extra = generate_seeds(n_full, rng, None)
    for cand in extra[:10]:
        if cand.size != n_full:
            cand = upsample_interp(cand, n_full)
        else:
            cand = normalize_nonneg(cand, 0.0)
        sc = compute_score(cand)
        candidates.append((sc, cand.copy()))

    candidates = sorted(candidates, key=lambda ze: ze[0])[:10]
    best_sc = candidates[0][0]
    best_a = candidates[0][1].copy()
    print(f"[init] best={best_sc:.8f} n={best_a.size}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase A: coarse LP search. This is the key SOTA-inspired phase.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    n_coarse = 768
    coarse_deadline = min(deadline - 1200.0, t0 + 650.0)
    coarse_parent = None
    if parent_full is not None:
        coarse_parent = downsample_sum(parent_full, n_coarse)
    coarse_seeds = generate_seeds(n_coarse, rng, coarse_parent)[:5]

    for i, cs in enumerate(coarse_seeds):
        if time.monotonic() > coarse_deadline - 90.0:
            break
        if cs.size != n_coarse:
            cs = upsample_interp(cs, n_coarse)
        tstart = time.monotonic()
        cf, csc = coarse_lp_search(cs, max_lp_calls=14, deadline=coarse_deadline - 45.0)
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - tstart
        # Upsample to full resolution.
        af = upsample_interp(cf, n_full)
        scf = compute_score(af)
        candidates.append((scf, af))
        print(f"[lp:{i}] coarse score={csc:.8f} full score={scf:.8f} time={elapsed:.1f}s")

    candidates = sorted(candidates, key=lambda ze: ze[0])[:12]
    best_sc = candidates[0][0]
    best_a = candidates[0][1].copy()
    print(f"[after LP] best={best_sc:.8f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase B: convolution-domain spectral shaping from best candidates.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    c_deadline = min(deadline - 900.0, t0 + 1050.0)
    cparams = [
        (0, 0.98, 0.88, 0.45, 0),
        (0, 0.96, 0.84, 0.60, 1),
        (1, 0.10, 0.55, 0.0, 0),
        (1, 0.25, 0.70, 0.0, 1),
        (2, 0.15, 0.60, 0.0, 0),
    ]

    for i in range(min(4, len(candidates))):
        if time.monotonic() > c_deadline - 30.0:
            break
        a0 = candidates[i][1]
        for mode, s1, s2, p2, pm in cparams:
            if time.monotonic() > c_deadline - 15.0:
                break
            b, bs = c_shape_cycle(a0, n_iters=90, mode=mode,
                                  p1_start=s1, p1_end=s2, p2=p2, phase_mode=pm)
            candidates.append((bs, b))
            if bs < best_sc:
                best_sc = bs
                best_a = b.copy()

    candidates = sorted(candidates, key=lambda ze: ze[0])[:12]
    best_sc = candidates[0][0]
    best_a = candidates[0][1].copy()
    print(f"[after C-shape] best={best_sc:.8f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase C: log-domain L-BFGS on smoothened max/Lp objectives.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    lp_deadline = deadline - 300.0
    eval_time = estimate_theta_eval_time(best_a.size, p=2.0)
    eval_time = max(eval_time, 1e-4)

    # (p, number_of_best_starts_to_try, seconds_allocated_per_call)
    schedule = [
        (2.0, 3, 260.0),
        (4.0, 3, 190.0),
        (8.0, 2, 130.0),
        (16.0, 2, 90.0),
        (32.0, 2, 70.0),
        (64.0, 1, 55.0),
    ]

    for p, tries, budget in schedule:
        if time.monotonic() > lp_deadline - 30.0:
            break
        candidates = sorted(candidates, key=lambda ze: ze[0])[:12]
        for ii in range(min(tries, len(candidates))):
            if time.monotonic() > lp_deadline - 25.0:
                break
            start_a = candidates[ii][1]
            chunk = max(45.0, min(budget, lp_deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0))
            if chunk < 40.0:
                break
            maxfun = int(chunk / max(eval_time, 1e-4) * 0.25)
            maxfun = max(350, min(20000, maxfun))

            res_a = log_lbfgs_run(start_a, p, maxfun=maxfun)
            sc = compute_score(res_a)
            candidates.append((sc, res_a))
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = res_a.copy()
                print(f"[lbfgs p={p:g} start={ii}] new best={sc:.8f}")

        candidates = sorted(candidates, key=lambda ze: ze[0])[:12]

    best_sc = candidates[0][0]
    best_a = candidates[0][1].copy()
    print(f"[after L-BFGS] best={best_sc:.8f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase D: final exact peak-subgradient polish on top candidates.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    final_deadline = deadline - 60.0
    candidates = sorted(candidates, key=lambda ze: ze[0])[:12]

    for i in range(min(3, len(candidates))):
        if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 30.0:
            break
        a0 = candidates[i][1]
        b, bs = peak_log_descent(a0, max_iters=260, n_peaks=14,
                                 lr_init=0.18, deadline=final_deadline - 20.0)
        candidates.append((bs, b))
        if bs < best_sc:
            best_sc = bs
            best_a = b.copy()
            print(f"[peak:{i}] new best={bs:.8f}")

    # If there is still time, one more high-p L-BFGS polish.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 90.0:
        maxfun = int(180.0 / max(eval_time, 1e-4) * 0.25)
        maxfun = max(500, min(15000, maxfun))
        res_a = log_lbfgs_run(best_a, 96.0, maxfun=maxfun)
        sc = compute_score(res_a)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = res_a.copy()
            print(f"[final lbfgs] new best={sc:.8f}")

    # Final peak polish if possible.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 25.0:
        b, bs = peak_log_descent(best_a, max_iters=120, n_peaks=8,
                                 lr_init=0.08, deadline=final_deadline - 10.0)
        if bs < best_sc:
            best_sc = bs
            best_a = b.copy()
            print(f"[final peak] new best={bs:.8f}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Output.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_sc) or best_a.size < 1:
        best_a = np.ones(n_full, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_full)
        best_sc = compute_score(best_a)

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_a = np.maximum(best_a, 0.0)
    s = float(best_a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        best_a = np.ones(n_full, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(best_a.size)
    best_a = best_a / s

    # Clip to evaluator limits.
    best_a = np.minimum(best_a, 1000.0)
    best_a = np.nan_to_num(best_a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)

    out = [float(x) for x in best_a]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float(best_sc)

    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"FINAL: internal={best_sc:.8f}, verified={verified:.8f}, n={len(out)}")
    print(f"TIME: {time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 60)

    return out
```
